# Fair Market Rents: Puerto Rico

- Postal code: PR
- State FIPS: 72
- Counties: 78
- Towns: 0

HUD's Fair Market Rents for Puerto Rico, fiscal years 1983 to 2027: 78 counties, each with its rents by bedroom count and the FMR areas it has sat in. 49 of them have changed FMR area at least once (not counting 2006, when HUD renumbered every area).
